## Text Elements
typedef enm{
    init,
    got1,
    got10
    got101
} 
state_t;

module moore_101(
    input clk,
    input reset,
    input n,
    output reg g 
);

state_t current=state , next_state
alsways@(postedge lk or postedge reset) begin
    if (reset) begin
        curent_state <= init
    end else begin 
        current_state <= next_state
    end
end

always @ (*) begin
    case (current_state)
        init = next_state = (x==1'b1)?got1:init;
        got1: next_state = (x==1'b0)?got10:got1;
        got10: next_state = (x==1'b1)?got1:got10;
            defualt : next_state = init;
        end case
end

always @(t) begin
    case(current_state)
        got101: g=1'b1;
        defualt : g=1'b0;
        endcase
   end
end module ^xkgPmPBC

module 32bit(
    input wire clk,
    input wire reset,
    input wire load,
    input wire [1:0] op,  //operation like + * ...
    input wire [31:0] data, //save data
    output reg [31:0] q //output
);

reg [31:0] reg_data;

always @(posedge clk or posedge reset) begin
    if (reset) begin
        reg_data <= 32'b0;
        q <= 32'b0;
    end else if (load)begin
        reg_data <= data;
        case(op)
            2'b00: q <= data +1 //increase
            2'b00: q <= data -1 //decrease
            2'b00: q <= data *5 //multiply 5
            2'b00: q <= data *7 //multiply 7
            default: q <= 32'b0;
        endcase
    end
   end

endmodule ^GESPLns9
